### ADR-071: Potency/effects/subcategory extraction — record-level identity, verbatim units
**Status**: Accepted (2026-07-03) — PR #55 open (`feat/potency-extraction`, `bfe6944`), not yet merged
**Context**: `data-collection-audit-2026-07-03` graded every THC/CBD insight a hard No while the Dutchie FilteredProducts payload already carries `THCContent`/`CBDContent` (`{unit, range}` self-describing), `totalTerpenes`, `effects`, `subcategory` — the extractor read none of them, and every un-captured day is lost data. First of three collection fixes (category expansion and deal-banner-history snapshot deferred to `deferred-work.md`).
**Decision**: Extract the five fields in `_dutchieProducts.ts` (single chokepoint: `potency()`, `effectsMap()`), carry through `RawProduct` → `normalizeProduct` (pure pass-through, no flags) → `ProductRecord` as **record-level descriptive identity** — optional (`?`) fields refreshed each scrape like `name`/`brand`, deliberately NOT per-observation. `PotencyRange {unit, low, high}`: unit stored VERBATIM (trimmed), never converted; unit-less or negative numbers → null (a number stripped of its stated unit lies; negative potency is impossible under any unit). Legacy scalar `THC`/`CBD` payload fields deliberately NOT read (undocumented unit). `totalTerpenes` is the one exception — a unit-undocumented scalar collected verbatim because no self-describing alternative exists (caveat documented in the type; do not compare across stores until the unit is established empirically). Weedmaps transform states explicit nulls (its potency out of scope). Effects map built via `Object.fromEntries` so a JSON-sourced `__proto__` key is stored, not swallowed.
**Rationale**: Record-level reuses the existing identity-refresh spread in `applyProductObservations` — zero store-code change, zero migration (next daily scrape backfills all ~3.6k committed records; proven by test), and ~5 small fields × records instead of × 23k+ observations on a file that grows daily via commit-back. Answers all of audit area 8 (THC per dollar, strongest products, THC by brand/store) once accrued.
**Consequences**: (1) Old potency is overwritten on refresh like `name`/`brand` — one scrape with missing/malformed `THCContent` nulls the record until the payload heals (~1 day at daily cadence); keep-last-known retention deferred as a policy call. (2) Committed dataset is three-state (`key absent` = pre-potency/delisted record, `null` = provider silent, value) — consumers use `== null`. (3) Magnitude-bounds policy (the "21.5% entered as 2150" POS-typo class) deferred until real out-of-range values appear. Both deferrals in `deferred-work.md` (2026-07-03 section).
**Testing**: 3-reviewer adversarial pass (acceptance auditor: compliant; hunter findings patched: unit trim, negative rejection, `__proto__` safety, end-to-end transform→normalize→merge chain test). Server suite **417 green** (37 files); full `npm run build` (client+server) clean.

---

### ADR-072: Category expansion — collect Edible + Concentrate, category-aware honest basis
**Status**: Accepted (2026-07-04) — PR #57 merged (`7fea084`)
**Context**: Collection fix #2 (audit gap G2). Only Flower/Vaporizers/Pre-Rolls were collected; Edible and Concentrate arrive in every Dutchie FilteredProducts payload (live capture 2026-06-24: singular `type` values) and were dropped at extraction. The derivation-engine PRD needs this data accruing. Blocker was unit honesty: an edible option label ("100mg") states mg-THC, not product weight — `parseGrams` would store a false weight and a ~10× inflated $/gram.
**Decision**: Widen `DEFAULT_PRODUCT_CATEGORIES` (Dutchie) and `CATEGORY_RULES`/slugs (Weedmaps) to the two confirmed categories, with a **category-aware basis** single-sourced as `WEIGHT_BASED_CATEGORIES` (exported from `normalizeProduct.ts` = Flower/Vaporizers/Pre-Rolls/Concentrate): Concentrate gets full existing weight math; Edible is collected verbatim with `weightGrams`/per-gram null and **no** `unparseable-weight` flag (n/a-not-defect, mirroring the pricePerItem precedent), and is excluded+counted from the disparity matcher via new `MatchReport.nonComparableCategoryCount` (matcher gate 5 — required because the matcher re-parses option labels itself). Three review-driven guards: (1) `BANNER_LINKABLE_CATEGORIES` filter in `dealScope.matchStoreProducts` keeps the deal→SKU bridge's output invariant (a storewide banner must not silently sweep new-category SKUs into links); (2) Concentrate match keys include `subcategory` so live-resin vs distillate with identical brand+strain cannot emit a false disparity (all other keys byte-identical); (3) Weedmaps edible rule ordered BEFORE vape/flower + word-bounded, blocking an Edibles-ancestor item ("flower-infused honey") from being misfiled weight-based.
**Rationale**: Collection-first — honest $/mg for edibles needs accrued potency (ADR-071) and belongs to the derivation engine; every un-collected day is lost longitudinal data. Verbatim option labels mean nothing is lost by not parsing them now.
**Consequences**: products.json grows (~3.6k → est. 5–6k records, ~14.8 → ~20–25 MB) on the same daily-append mechanics. Edibles produce zero disparities and zero banner links until deliberately enabled (concentrate banner cues + edible linking deferred in `deferred-work.md` 2026-07-04, alongside the pre-existing "concentrate ounces"→Flower@28g false friend). `ScrapedCategory` stays 3-wide by design (banner-linkable ≠ collected).
**Testing**: 3-reviewer pass (auditor: compliant; 6 patches applied incl. the two guards above). Server suite **436 green**; audit test proves zero behavior change for committed records; full `npm run build` clean. Post-merge check: next scrape-products run should commit the first Edible/Concentrate records.

---

### ADR-073: Rotating sale-tag art pools (edible ×11, bud ×4) + PR #58 squash-drop recovery
**Status**: Accepted (2026-07-04) — **Amended same day**: `vape` ×3 (`Vape Cartridge*` JPEGs) and `shatter` ×2 (`SHATTER/`) pools added on the unchanged mechanism. Shatter became a first-class `DealIconName` split out of the dabs family (`\bshatter\b` now matches most-specific-first ahead of diamond/dabs; one-concentrate-glyph rule intact; `shatter-01` doubles as the underflow fallback since the family never had a legacy single asset). The vestigial `_assertIcons` Record in `dealIcons.ts` (compile-proof that every name had an Icon.tsx SVG glyph) was retired — deal tags render Erik's art via `DEAL_ICON_SRC`, whose `Record<DealIconName, string>` typing already fails the build on a missing name, and 'shatter' never had an SVG glyph. Spec: `spec-vape-shatter-icon-rotation.md`.
**Context**: Every edible-matched deal showed the same single `edible.webp` tag; Erik supplied 11 edible art pieces (`Happy-assets/GmasINCOlist/Store sale tags/edds`) and, mid-implementation, 4 bud/flower pieces (`…/BUDS FLOWER ICONS`), asking that each matching deal draw a different image — no repeats until a pool is exhausted, reshuffled each cycle, assigned top-to-bottom in feed order. Constraint discovered in planning: `DealFeed` re-renders every second (`useNow` countdowns), so naive `Math.random()` shuffling would visibly re-deal icons each tick.
**Decision**: New `client/src/utils/dealIconPools.ts` — family-keyed `ROTATING_ICON_POOLS` (`edible`, `bud`) + pure `buildIconSequence(pool, count, rng)` producing concatenated Fisher-Yates shuffles (every aligned pool-sized window repeat-free; generation prefix-stable so growing counts never re-deal earlier entries) + `mulberry32` seeded PRNG. `DealFeed` holds one `useState` seed per mount, counts each family's glyph occurrences per store via the same `buildDealBlocks` the cards render from, and slices one feed-wide sequence into a per-store `rotatingIconSrcs` prop; `DealCard` consumes srcs in block reading order, falling back to the legacy single art on underflow. Matcher (`dealIcons.ts`) untouched — `drink`/`tincture`/etc. keep dedicated art. Assets: 15 new webps via the ADR-067 ImageMagick recipe (edible-09 needed a gentler `-fuzz 2%` trim + own-background padding — its white lollipop stick sat on off-white and the standard 10% fuzz erased it). **Same PR recovers PR #58's squash-dropped car commit** (`e1e1be6` cherry-picked): the #58 squash captured only the pin-art commit, leaving `VehicleBar`/`VehicleSelector` on the old stroke glyph — exactly the [[feedback_verify-squash-merge-fileset]] failure mode again; Erik's miles-slider car art now renders and the `car` glyph is removed from `Icon.tsx`.
**Rationale**: Seeded-PRNG-per-mount is the only design satisfying both "shuffled each cycle" and "stable across per-second re-renders" without persisting rotation state; feed-level assignment (not per-card) is required for the no-repeat cycle to span cards; prefix-stability makes late-arriving deals additive instead of reshuffling what's on screen.
**Consequences**: A filter change that removes earlier edible/bud deals shifts later assignments (position-based) — accepted in spec. Each visit shuffles fresh (no cross-session persistence, by design). Adding a rotation family = drop assets + one entry in `ROTATING_ICON_POOLS`.
**Testing**: 530 client tests green (13 new: pool invariants — chunk distinctness, cycle reshuffle, prefix stability, determinism; DealCard — src consumption order, underflow fallback, non-pool families never rotate). Full `npm run build` clean. Spec: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-edible-icon-rotation.md`.

### ADR-074: Deal-icon fallthrough reduction — order-scope → store-wide glyph, Shatterday, firecrackers/sparklers
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-05
**Context**: Erik flagged (screenshot, gmaslist.com) that the generic `special-pricing` sale-tag was carrying too many deals — 22 of 45 unique live deals fell through to it, including deals a human reads as clearly categorizable ("Shatterday", "40% Off Online Orders", "FIRECRACKERS+SPARKLERS"), while ~30 art pieces sat available. "Cheap and sloppy."
**Decision**: Three matcher additions in `dealIcons.ts`, no new icon names: (1) shatter family regex widened to `\bshatter(?:day)?s?\b` so "Shatterday" matches; (2) new `ORDER_SCOPE` pattern (`online orders`, `your purchase`, `items purchased`, `single purchases`, `customer groups`) routed to the exclusive `store-wide` glyph — these discount the *whole order*, product-agnostic, the exact scope the globe already means; (3) `firecrackers`/`sparklers` added to the pre-roll family (infused pre-roll products, July-4th naming). "GLASS" (paraphernalia) deliberately left on the generic tag: no glassware art exists — mapping it to any product glyph would violate ADR-067's honest-match rule. Erik AFK; defaults chosen per the ratified-AFK-defaults pattern, glass art offered as follow-up.
**Rationale**: ORDER_SCOPE kept narrow (no bare `\bpurchases?\b`) so "20% off vape purchases" still matches vape, not store-wide. Reusing the store-wide glyph avoids inventing a new icon for a scope the globe already communicates.
**Consequences**: Generic-tag fallthrough drops 22 → ~12 live deals; the remainder ("Select Products/Brands", "Rotating Brands", glass, membership) genuinely name no product, so the tag is honest there. Firecracker-as-edible (the homemade cracker snack) is a theoretical false friend; retail listings use the term for infused pre-rolls.
**Testing**: 10 new/updated cases in `dealIcons.test.ts` (order-scope table incl. all four live online-orders strings, Shatterday, firecrackers, vape-purchases guard, glass stays generic); full client suite 540 green; full `npm run build` clean.

### ADR-075: Glass (paraphernalia) icon family — 4-art rotation pool
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-05
**Context**: ADR-074 left "July Glass Sale *30% OFF GLASS*" on the generic sale-tag because no glassware art existed. Erik supplied 4 pieces (`Happy-assets/GmasINCOlist/Store sale tags/Glass sale icons/`) and asked they rotate "like we do with the randoming, use them all."
**Decision**: New `glass` `DealIconName` (label "Glassware") + PRODUCT_FAMILIES entry `\bglass(?:ware)?\b|\bbongs?\b|\bdab rigs?\b|\bwater pipes?\b|\bhand pipes?\b` — bare "pipes"/"rigs" deliberately excluded as too ambiguous. Two collision guards: FALSE_FRIENDS gains `glass house` (Glass House Farms is a flower brand), and the dabs regex gains a lookahead `\bdabs?\b(?!\s?rigs?\b)` so "dab rigs" reads as glassware, not a dab product. Assets: 4 webps at `client/src/assets/deal-icons/glass/glass-01..04.webp` (bong, spoon pipe, two twisted-pipe tiles) via the ADR-067 recipe + `-quality 55` (gradient tiles compressed 9–24KB → 1.3–3.4KB; all <4KB so Vite inlines them as data URIs — verify deploys by bundle content). Rotation: one `ROTATING_ICON_POOLS.glass` entry (ADR-073 mechanism, zero mechanism change); `glass-01` doubles as the underflow fallback in `DEAL_ICON_SRC`, same pattern as shatter.
**Rationale**: Only honest way to icon a paraphernalia sale is dedicated glassware art; the pool mechanism was built to take new families as data.
**Consequences**: Generic-tag fallthrough drops another deal (~12 → ~11). ADR-074's "glass stays generic" note is superseded.
**Testing**: 5 new matcher cases (GLASS sale, glassware, bongs, water pipes + dab rigs → glass only; Glass House brand → bud) + pool-shape assertions updated. Full client suite 544 green; full `npm run build` clean.

### ADR-076: Icon category filter bar replaces Deal.type chips
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-05
**Context**: The feed's only filter was three text chips over `Deal.type` (All deals / Happy hours / Daily deals) — a field shoppers don't think in. Deal cards already tag every deal with product-category icons (ADR-067..075 art + `dealIcons` matcher), but the categories weren't actionable. Erik asked for the chips to be replaced by those same icons, shown only when present on-page, filtering on click.
**Decision**: `DealTypeFilter` deleted (with `filterByType`/`DealTypeSelection`); new `DealCategoryFilter` renders icon toggle chips wearing each family's canonical `DEAL_ICON_SRC` art (rotation pools stay card-only). Erik's explicit calls: click = FILTER (feed narrows to stores with a matching active deal, matching deals only within each card; re-click clears), ALL tags qualify (incl. store-wide / price-drop / special-pricing), joint pack variants collapse into one pre-roll icon. Mechanics in `dealView.ts`: `DealCategory`, `dealCategories(deal)` (the SAME `dealIcons` matcher on `buildDealBlocks`' layered-sale-aware subject — bar and card tags cannot disagree), `categoriesPresent` (an icon renders only when ≥1 post-stale/distance/expiry store carries it, computed PRE-filter so pressing one icon never hides the others), `filterByCategory`. `CATEGORY_ORDER` is Record-typed so an unslotted future `DealIconName` fails the build instead of becoming silently unfilterable.
**Rationale**: One matcher source of truth was the load-bearing constraint — any second categorization path could show an icon the cards don't (or vice versa). Presence-before-filter keeps the bar stable under selection. `Deal.type` filtering retired rather than kept alongside: two filter vocabularies on one row confuse more than they help.
**Consequences**: Happy-hour/daily filtering is gone (the cards still badge urgency). Matcher false-negatives now hide deals under a selection instead of just mis-tagging a visible card. ~~A store-wide-only deal hides under a product icon~~ — resolved same day by the store-wide WILDCARD (Erik's call): `filterByCategory` matches store-wide deals under every PRODUCT selection (a "25% off everything" deal does discount vapes), while scope selections (store-wide/price-drop/special-pricing) stay exact. Same follow-up PR: visible bold titles under each bar icon (the label IS the accessible name — aria-label dropped) and canonical glass art swapped glass-01 → glass-02 (spoon pipe on white; the green-bong-on-green tile was muddy at 28px).
**Testing**: 3-reviewer adversarial pass (blind hunter / edge-case hunter / acceptance auditor). One major fixed: a departed category's selection is CLEARED via render-phase state write, never masked (a masked selection silently re-engaged the filter when the category returned — resurrection-leg test added; note `useNow` ticks every 60s, not 1s). 554 client tests green; full `npm run build` clean; deploy verified live by bundle-content grep. PR #63 → master `9ce77fb`.

---

### ADR-077: Products dataset → local SQLite + push-derived-facts (retires products.json commit-back)
**Status**: Accepted — **Phase 1 IMPLEMENTED 2026-07-06** (substrate + derive + repoint + `git rm products.json` landed; plan in `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/plans/products-storage-local-sqlite-plan.md`). Phase 0 (mtime-cache) + Phase 2 (hardening) remain separate tracks.
**Date**: 2026-07-06
**Context**: The 2026-07-05 data-pipeline forensic audit (Finding 8) measured `server/data/products.json` at 18.8 MB growing ~1.75 MB/day (5,090 records / 29,669 obs). It is a single append-only JSON committed back to git each scrape (ADR-053) because Render's disk is ephemeral. Two failure modes: **P1** — GitHub warns at 50 MB (~2026-07-23) and hard-blocks push at 100 MB (~2026-08-20), after which the dataset silently stops accruing (workflow only emits `::warning::` on push failure). **P2** — `readProducts()` parses the whole file UNCACHED on every `/api/products` + `/api/value/*` request → 3–5× heap blow-up, OOM risk on Render free (512 MB). Committing a growing dataset to git is using version control as a database — a bridge, not a destination. Pruning history is forbidden (the longitudinal asset feeds B1 trendlines + Phase-4). ADR-033 (paid Docker self-host) is parked and NOT to be revived.
**Decision**: Raw longitudinal history moves OUT of git into a **local SQLite DB on Erik's home machine** (the machine already running the residential Weedmaps scrape). The public Render site serves only small, pre-computed **derived facts** — the `MatchReport` (disparities) and `DealScopeReport` — which the home machine computes locally and commits back. **Load-bearing rule: Render never queries the home DB live** — if the home machine is off, the site keeps serving the last-pushed facts; only accrual pauses, never site availability. Erik OWNS backup of the raw DB (his explicit accepted trade-off, in exchange for killing the file-size wall permanently and keeping the proprietary raw asset on hardware he controls). Phased: **P0** mtime-cache `readProducts()` now (de-risk P2, independent); **P1** import products.json→`products.db`, add a local derivation runner (mirrors `scrape-weedmaps-local.ps1`), repoint value routes to derived files, `git rm products.json` (kills the wall) — before ~2026-07-23; **P2** move Dutchie scraping fully local + retire the Actions products cron, add the backup routine, add SQLite indices when B1 needs time-range queries.
**Rationale**: Boring + reversible + $0. The server change is minimal because the value routes already serialize exactly what `buildMatchReport`/`buildDealScopeLinks` return (`valueRoute.ts:23,52`) — "serve derived facts" is just reading those objects from a file instead of computing them, so honesty gates (fix6, `EXCLUDED_FLAGS`, Gates 1–5) and all types are untouched. Local self-host fits an EXISTING pattern (the residential Scheduled Task) rather than introducing a new paradigm, and is categorically distinct from the parked *paid* Docker self-host (ADR-033). Rejected alternatives: keep-with-sharding (Option ①) is a good 12–18-month BRIDGE but still git-as-DB — kept only as an optional stepping stone (P0's cache is worth it regardless); SQLite-committed-to-git worsens `.git` bloat (binary blob, no delta) and still marches to the same wall; Render Postgres free expires ~30 days (needs paid → collides with ADR-033) and forfeits the git-as-export-format property.
**Consequences**: Kills the P1 git wall permanently and the P2 OOM risk. Erik takes on backup responsibility (git's free offsite backup is given up — Phase-2 target TBD: private-repo git-LFS / object storage / drive-sync). Two-machine mental model; Dutchie scraping eventually needs the Python `scraper-svc` running locally (Weedmaps is a static fetch, doesn't). `/api/products` (ships the whole blob) is dropped or bounded — open decision. deal-scope precomputed daily → up to ~24 h staleness vs today's per-request freshness (open decision: accept, or ship a small per-store SKU index for live compute). Deals pipeline (`data.json`, `/api/data`) untouched (ADR-043/053 preserved). Open decisions + parity tests enumerated in the plan doc.
**Testing**: Not yet implemented. Planned gates: import parity (record/obs counts = 5,090 / 29,669, zero loss); derived-fact parity (disparities from `products.db` == the live 217; `excludedFlagCount` incl. new `unreconciled-pack`, `nonComparableCategoryCount`, deal-scope buckets sum to total deals); fail-soft on missing derived files; `npm run build` clean.

**Phase 1 implementation (2026-07-06):**
- **Driver:** `node:sqlite` (Node 24 built-in, synchronous) — chosen over `better-sqlite3` for zero native dependency, no node-gyp/prebuild risk on Windows, and it can never enter the client bundle. No package.json change.
- **Schema** (`server/utils/productsDb.ts`): `product(product_key PK, dispensaryId, productId, name, category, brand, strainType, packCount, thc, cbd, totalTerpenes, effects, subcategory, flags)` — thc/cbd/effects/flags JSON-encoded text; `observation(id PK, product_key FK, dispensaryId, observedAt, special, options_json)`. Indices: **UNIQUE `(product_key, observedAt)`** (the AC2 per-store/per-day presence key + integrity guard — source had 0 dup pairs), `(observedAt)` and denormalized `(dispensaryId, observedAt)` for the derivation engine's regional/time-range facts. Reconstruction preserves observation insertion order so `history.at(-1)` (the only access the matcher/dealScope make) is the true latest → **gap-tolerant** (absent day ≠ unchanged day).
- **Migration proven zero-loss** against the real 21.7 MB file: **5,219 records / 33,169 observations** both sides; `buildMatchReport(DB)` byte-equals `buildMatchReport(JSON)` → **228 disparities** (matching live prod exactly), excluded 568, nonComparable 510, unmatched 1; `buildDealScopeLinks` byte-equal too. (Audit-time 5,090/29,669/217 were stale estimates — asserted against the live file per AC1.)
- **Seam** (`readProductsFile`): returns the exact `ProductsFile` shape, so `buildMatchReport`/`buildDealScopeLinks` stay **byte-identical** (untouched). New CLIs: `importProductsToSqlite.ts` (asserts count parity), `deriveFactsRun.ts` (DB → `server/data/derived/{disparities,deal-scope}.json`).
- **Routes repointed** (`valueRoute.ts`): `/api/value/disparities` + `/api/value/deal-scope` read the committed derived JSON, **fail-soft to empty** (never throw, never open the home DB — the load-bearing rule). `crossStoreValue.audit.test.ts` rebased onto the committed derived file.
- **Feed the DB (AC7):** `scrapeProductsRun.ts` (Dutchie) + `scrapeWeedmapsRun.ts` (Weedmaps) gained an injectable `persist` sink; their CLIs now append into `products.db` (`persistObservationsToDb`, append-only + idempotent via the UNIQUE index). Local orchestration: `scripts/derive-facts-local.ps1` (commit-back of derived only, mirrors the residential runner's clean-base reset), `scripts/scrape-dutchie-local.ps1` (boots the vendored Python `scraper-svc`), and `scripts/scrape-weedmaps-local.ps1` (rewired from JSON commit-back to DB append). DB lives at `PRODUCTS_DB_PATH` (default `~/GmaS-data/products.db`) OUTSIDE any worktree so `git reset --hard` can't touch it. Runbook: `docs/products-local-sqlite-ingest.md`.
- **Wall killed:** `git rm server/data/products.json` (backed up to `~/GmaS-data/backups/` first — raw JSON + first DB); retired `.github/workflows/scrape-products.yml` (Dutchie fully local now). `products.db` git-ignored.
- **Resolved open decisions:** #1 → **Dutchie moved fully local**; #2 → **`/api/products` dropped entirely** (route + test removed); #3 → **deal-scope precomputed daily** (accept ~24h staleness). Server suite **449 tests green**; `npm run build` clean.
- **Still operational (Erik-run, not yet live — same posture as the Weedmaps residential runner at merge):** first `pwsh scripts/scrape-dutchie-local.ps1` / `scrape-weedmaps-local.ps1` + `derive-facts-local.ps1` passes from the home machine to confirm accrual + commit-back. Phase-2 checkpoint: `caravan-cannabis-burlington` suspected silent-extraction failure to resolve before "one machine owns all raw data".
- **Code-review hardening (2026-07-07):** the 3-layer adversarial review caught one real load-bearing gap — `readProductsFromDbPath` was fail-soft (mirroring `readProducts()`), but reused as the derivation runner's INPUT, so a misconfigured `PRODUCTS_DB_PATH` or a DB-open failure could have silently produced an empty `ProductsFile` that `derive-facts-local.ps1` would then commit+push over the live derived facts. Fixed: the DB read now throws on failure, and `deriveFacts()` refuses to overwrite a previously-populated derived file with a zero-record result (the fail-soft posture stays correct in `valueRoute.ts`, which only ever reads already-computed files). Also fixed: AC8's `buildDealScopeLinks` parity was asserted by hand during dev but never captured as a test (added); the importer's DROP-and-recreate could destroy accrued observations if re-run against a stale backup (now requires `--force`); no WAL/busy_timeout for the three concurrent local writers (added); `readDerived` accepted any parseable JSON including wrong-shaped objects/arrays (added a shape check); a duplicate-day retry could clobber good product metadata with degraded data (fixed ordering); first-run setup had no parent-dir creation; `scrape-dutchie-local.ps1` didn't surface uvicorn logs on failure or kill orphaned Chromium children. 459 server tests green (+10); build clean.

### ADR-078: AI-search entity surface — server-rendered /about + FAQ + JSON-LD entity markup
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-10
**Context**: The site is invisible to search/AI crawlers (SEO spec `spec-seo-crawler-visibility`: zero pages indexed; Express serves an empty SPA shell). Erik drafted an entity strategy in four Obsidian vault notes (GMASLIST folder): AI search matches *intent*, so gmaslist.com must be legible as one canonical entity — "independent consumer information service, not a cannabis seller" — and answer intent questions ("is this deal worth the drive?") in extractable text. His raw FAQ draft repeated "does not sell cannabis" 6×, had typos, proposed a SearchAction schema for a nonexistent /search, and set `areaServed` to the whole US.
**Decision**: Ship a dedicated **server-rendered `/about` page** (plain HTML template built at module load in `server/routes/aboutRoute.ts`, registered before the production SPA fallback, unconditional in dev; Cache-Control 1h) carrying the polished About sections + a 9-question FAQ, with **Service + FAQPage JSON-LD**; add **WebSite + Organization JSON-LD** + a meta description to `client/index.html`; add a one-line identity statement + /about link to `DisclaimerFooter`; proxy `/about` in Vite dev. Content is consolidated/polished from the vault notes with deliberate deviations: SearchAction **dropped** (no /search exists — schema must not lie), `areaServed` = **Washington State** (WAC 314-55-155 local-only), the not-a-seller negation stated once per relevant answer (repetition strengthens the wrong "Gmas List"↔"sell cannabis" co-occurrence in retrieval embeddings), positive identity always leads. **Load-bearing rule: `FAQ_ITEMS`/`ENTITY_DESCRIPTION` are the single source for both visible HTML and JSON-LD**, so Google's schema-must-match-page requirement holds by construction (tests enforce word-for-word).
**Rationale**: A static entity page is the cheapest crawlable surface (no Phase 0a data injection needed, zero SPA/age-gate coupling) and the FAQ shape is exactly what intent-matching AI search extracts. Kept fully decoupled from deal data so it can never go stale with the pipeline.
**Consequences**: /about is the first real (non-shell) HTML the server emits; Phase 0a (deal-data injection), robots.txt/sitemap (Phase 1), and per-store routes (1a) remain separate stories. Deferred (deferred-work.md): whether the page needs a 21+/WA-warning statement (counsel call); Organization `logo`/`sameAs` enrichment. JSON-LD `<` is emitted as a unicode escape so copy can never terminate the script element; index.html's hand-written JSON-LD is guarded by a parse-validity test (`client/src/indexHtml.test.ts`).
**Testing**: 3-reviewer adversarial pass (acceptance auditor: fully compliant; 4 patches applied — dev proxy, script-element hardening, index.html JSON-LD test, Cache-Control). Server 573 + client 561 green; full `npm run build` clean; compiled-server smoke: `GET /about` = 200 text/html with both JSON-LD blocks, `/api/data` unaffected.

---

### ADR-079: Public derived-facts surface — server-rendered /compare comparison pages + Dataset JSON-LD (derivation-3.1)
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-10
**Context**: Derivation-engine Epic 1 produces honest cross-store price-disparity facts (`server/data/derived/disparities.json`, `disparity-rollups.json`) but serves them only on private JSON API routes — no public page, no structured data — so AI-search crawlers and Google cannot discover or cite them. ADR-066 closed the public-surfacing legal gate. Epic 3 ("Feed the surfaces") was undecomposed and gated on (a) that legal clearance [closed] and (b) Epic 2's FR13 honest-discount history [parked]. Sliced via bmad-quick-dev into two independent goals — SEO comparison pages + in-app value cards — and Erik chose the SEO surface first (the unblocked, highest-value one; the value cards' flagship "vs own history" card needs the parked Epic 2).
**Decision**: Ship server-rendered **`/compare` (index) + `/compare/:category`** pages (`server/routes/compareRoute.ts`, registered before the production SPA fallback, `/compare` added to the Vite dev proxy) that render the shipped Epic-1 disparity facts as extractable HTML + **`schema.org/Dataset` JSON-LD** — deliberately **NOT** commerce `Product`/`Offer`/`AggregateOffer` (a Google-restricted category for cannabis with real risk and little upside, and it implies the aggregator sells / reproduces menus). Read-only over the committed derived artifacts via the existing `readDerived` fail-soft envelope machinery — no recompute, additive, Render never opens the home DB. Mirrors the ADR-078 `/about` SSR pattern (single-source-of-truth constants so schema text = on-page text; JSON-LD `<`→`<`; canonical/title/meta description; `Cache-Control: public, max-age=3600`). Discovery via an internal link on `/about` (no sitemap yet — SEO-plan Phase 1). **Honesty contract enforced**: same-product-only (G1, `matchKey`), no discount % (G2 — renders cross-store price RANGES, not "how good a deal is"), no potency (G5), WA-only `spatialCoverage` (WAC 314-55-155), facts-not-menus.
**Rationale**: `Dataset` frames the honest comparison facts as a published dataset (transform-not-copy) — the AI-search moat and highest information-gain surface, with none of the commerce-schema risk. Reusing the `/about` SSR + `readDerived` patterns kept the change small, decoupled, and fail-soft. Stable-entity URLs only (index + per-category); no per-deal/per-product URLs, which churn hourly/daily into thin, rotting pages.
**Consequences**: `/compare` is the second server-rendered content class after `/about`. The in-app value cards (the other Epic-3 goal) are deferred behind Epic 2 FR13 (`deferred-work.md`); sitemap/robots/OG (SEO-plan Phase 1) remain separate. **Consumes existing derived artifacts — adds NO new `server/data/derived/*.json`, so the `derive-facts-local.ps1` `$derivedFiles` republish rule did not apply.** A future comparison surface over a NEW derived fact would need that runner change.
**Testing**: 3-reviewer adversarial pass (blind / edge-case / acceptance) — all findings patch or reject, no loopback. Load-bearing catch: a tie/zero-spread mislabel (~40% of live rows where `highPrice===lowPrice`) rendered "$35 at StoreA to $35 at StoreA" (argmin/argmax both return the first store) → fixed to "$X at all N stores"; plus price-pulled-from-named-store, single-source category validation (index links vs route validity — no dead links), inner-shape 500 guards (upholds the "never a 500" contract), attribute-safe escaping, top-100 row cap (NFR2), numeric exclusion accounting on the index (AC3). 583 server tests green (10 in `compareRoute.test.ts`); full `npm run build` clean. Merged PR#78 → master `9cbb672`; live-verified on gmaslist.com (`/compare` 200 with `Dataset` JSON-LD + real exclusion counts; `/compare/vaporizers` 79 spread + 21 tie rows; `/compare/nope` 404).

---

### ADR-080: Phase 1 technical-SEO foundation — robots.txt + dynamic sitemap.xml over the shipped surfaces
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-10
**Context**: `/about` (ADR-078) and `/compare` + `/compare/:category` (ADR-079) are server-rendered and crawlable, but the only path to them is an internal `/about`→`/compare` link. A crawler that never lands on `/about` can't discover them, and there is no `robots.txt`/`sitemap.xml` at all (SEO-plan Phase 1, confirmed zero pages indexed via `site:gmaslist.com`). This is the highest-leverage remaining SEO step after Phase 0a/entity surfaces.
**Decision**: Serve `robots.txt` and `sitemap.xml` as **Express routes** (`server/routes/sitemapRoute.ts`), registered before the production SPA fallback + `express.static` (same reason as `/about`/`/compare`: the `/^(?!\/api).*/` catch-all would otherwise return the React shell), and added to the Vite dev proxy. `robots.txt` allows all crawlers (Phase 3 decision: citation-search AND training bots), `Disallow: /api/`, advertises the sitemap. `sitemap.xml` lists the static entity pages (`/`, `/about`, `/compare`) plus one `/compare/<category>` URL per live category — enumerated from the **same `disparity-rollups.json` `byCategory` the `/compare` index links from** (via `readDerived` fail-soft + the shared `categorySlug`), so a sitemap entry can never point at a slug `compareCategoryRoute` 404s. No per-deal/per-store URLs (deals churn hourly; per-store routes are Phase 1a, not built).
**Rationale**: A dynamic route beats a static `client/public/sitemap.xml` because the category set is data-derived and grows — a committed static file would drift out of sync with the live `/compare` categories and risk listing dead or missing URLs. Single-source enumeration (rollups `byCategory`) makes index links, category routes, and sitemap entries provably consistent. `lastmod` is the honest artifact `generatedAt` on the data-backed `/compare` URLs only; static entity pages carry no fabricated lastmod.
**Consequences**: Crawlers get a complete map of every indexable page without depending on the internal discovery link. Fail-soft: a missing/malformed rollups artifact degrades to a sitemap of just the static URLs, never a 500. OG/Twitter meta tags + `LocalBusiness` per-store JSON-LD (rest of Phase 1) and per-store routes (Phase 1a) remain separate, unbuilt. Submitting the sitemap to Google/Bing Search Console (Phase 6) is an outward-facing action left to Erik.
**Testing**: New `sitemapRoute.test.ts` (11 tests): robots allow/deny/sitemap-line + single-User-agent (no per-bot blocks), sitemap valid XML + lists the three static surfaces + one URL per live rollup category, no per-deal URLs; pure `buildSitemapXml` — static-always-present, generatedAt lastmod on data-backed URLs only, slug de-dupe on collision, XML escaping. 594 server tests green (was 583); full `npm run build` clean.

### ADR-081: Lighthouse remediation — llms.txt route, skeleton CLS reservation, async font CSS
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-10
**Context**: First post-Phase-1 Lighthouse run against live `gmaslist.com` (mobile, 2026-07-10): SEO/accessibility/best-practices all 100, but performance 54 and agentic-browsing 43. Three root causes: (1) the SPA fallback served the React shell at `/llms.txt`, failing the llms-txt audit (no H1, no links); (2) CLS 0.367, almost entirely one shift — the footer paints in-viewport during the 3-row skeleton phase, then the arriving deal cards shove it ~6,500px down; (3) the Google Fonts stylesheet `<link>` render-blocked first paint ~870ms on throttled mobile (FCP 4.1s / LCP 4.9s).
**Decision**: (1) Serve `/llms.txt` as an Express route in `sitemapRoute.ts` (`buildLlmsTxt` + `llmsTxtRoute`, registered before the SPA fallback, added to the Vite dev proxy): llmstxt.org-format Markdown — H1, blockquote summary reusing `ENTITY_DESCRIPTION` from `aboutRoute.ts`, Pages section (`/`, `/about`, `/compare`), and one `/compare/<category>` link per live rollup category from the same `disparity-rollups.json` source as the sitemap. (2) `minHeight: '100vh'` on DealFeed's loading-skeleton wrapper so the footer starts below the fold and the skeleton→content swap shifts nothing in-viewport. (3) Async font pattern in `client/index.html`: `preload as="style"` + `media="print" onload="this.media='all'"` + `<noscript>` fallback; `display=swap` already in the URL keeps text visible on fallback fonts.
**Rationale**: Same single-source principle as ADR-080 — llms.txt category links, sitemap entries, and `/compare` index links all enumerate one artifact, so none can 404 or disagree. The CLS fix reserves space instead of restructuring the feed; it targets the one shift worth 0.366 of the 0.367 total. Async fonts is the standard non-blocking pattern; self-hosting fonts was rejected as a larger change for marginal extra gain.
**Consequences**: Agentic-browsing should reach ~100 (its three weighted audits: accessibility-tree already passing, llms-txt fixed, CLS fixed). Performance improves (CLS 25-point weight → ~100; ~870ms off the render-critical path) but will NOT hit 100: FCP/LCP remain SPA-bound (JS → `/api/data` fetch → render). Getting LCP under ~2.5s needs the deferred Phase 0a deal-data injection / SSR of the feed shell — that is the remaining performance lever, not micro-optimization. Rare "No active deals" responses now swap a 100vh skeleton for a short page (footer shifts up); accepted, the loaded feed is ~7,000px in practice. Font swap-flash on slow connections is unchanged (`display=swap` behavior).
**Testing**: 6 new tests in `sitemapRoute.test.ts` (route: plain-text + cache header, H1-first-line + blockquote + static links, one link per live category; pure builder: zero-category degradation, slug de-dupe/empty-slug drop, WA-only scope line). 600 server + 561 client tests green; full `npm run build` clean; `media="print" onload` pattern verified intact in built `client/dist/index.html`.

### ADR-082: Phase 0a perf half — /api/data snapshot injected into the served SPA shell; crawler-HTML half deferred (0b-gated)
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-11
**Context**: After ADR-081, 4 of 5 Lighthouse categories sit at 100; the only weighted failures are FCP 3.0s / LCP 4.9s. The live report's LCP element is a deal-card `h2`, reached via JS boot → `/api/data` fetch → render — the exact chain the SEO plan's Phase 0a targets. Full Phase 0a also wants crawler-readable deal HTML in the shell, but any human-visible deal markup paints before the JS-mounted AgeGate, which is the Phase 0b legal question (out of scope, Erik's explicit constraint).
**Decision**: Split Phase 0a. Ship the perf half now: Express injects the current `/api/data` payload as an inline `window.__GMA_DATA__` script into the served shell (`server/routes/shellRoute.ts`, `makeShellRoute(clientDist)` factory) for `/` and every SPA-fallback path; `express.static` gets `index: false` so the injected route is the only shell server. The `/api/data` derivation moved to a shared `server/utils/buildApiData.ts` consumed by both `dataRoute` and the injection, so the two can never disagree. `useDeals` initializes synchronously from the snapshot through the same shape guard + `normalizeDispensaries` boundary and skips the fetch; absent/invalid snapshot falls back to today's fetch path (dev unchanged). JSON is XSS-hardened by unicode-escaping every `<` (backslash-u003c). On any snapshot-build failure the plain shell is served (never a 500). Defer the crawler-visible HTML half to deferred-work.md pending the 0b legal call.
**Rationale**: The plan doc's own "lightest option". The injected JSON is exactly what `/api/data` already serves publicly and un-gated, so this adds zero new legal exposure — unlike rendered deal HTML, which is 0b's question. First data-bearing React render now needs no network round trip, collapsing LCP toward FCP; the skeleton phase disappears for shell-served loads.
**Consequences**: Expected LCP ~4.9s → ~FCP (~3.0s); FCP itself stays bundle-bound — full 100 performance still needs pre-JS paint (the deferred SSR half). Snapshot staleness ceiling = page-load age (DealFeed may mount minutes later post-gate; accepted — reload refreshes). Non-JS crawlers still see no deal content: Phase 0a's visibility goal remains open until 0b is answered. `Cache-Control: no-cache` on the shell prevents stale-deal caching.
**Testing**: 7 new tests in `shellRoute.test.ts` (injection + deep-equal parity against `buildApiData`, exactly-one script, deep-link injection, `<` escaping round-trip against a hostile `</script>` store name, `$`-pattern + U+2028 round-trip, data-unreadable AND data-malformed → plain 200 shell, missing shell → 500); 3 new `useDeals` tests (valid snapshot → synchronous data + zero fetch; non-object + malformed-shape snapshots → fetch fallback). 3-reviewer adversarial pass caught one real latent defect pre-merge — `String.replace` `$`-pattern expansion could let scraped text like `SAVE $$$` corrupt the snapshot or splice an unescaped `</head>` into the script — fixed with a function replacement + hostile-fixture test; also patched: `/index.html` now routes to the injected shell (static `index:false` alone didn't cover the literal path), missing-`</head>` marker logs instead of silently no-oping, U+2028/9 unicode-escaped, snapshot+fetch validation unified in one `validateApiData`. Deferred (deferred-work.md): snapshot remount-staleness TTL, payload-size growth bound. 607 server + 564 client green; full `npm run build` clean; compiled prod server curl-verified locally (snapshot parses, no raw `<`, `/` + `/index.html` + deep link all inject, static assets + `/api/data` + `/robots.txt` unaffected).

---

### ADR-083: Confirmed-empty ingest outcome — a provably dealless store clears + advances freshness instead of freezing stale
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-11
**Context**: `happy-time-mt-vernon` legitimately removed all its Dutchie specials when its June month-end promos expired (2026-06-30, live-verified 2026-07-11), but "store has zero specials" was unrepresentable: `scraperClient` collapsed service failures and genuine empties to the same `[]`, and `applyIngest`'s last-known-good rule never advances `lastFetchedAt` on an empty push. Result: the store redded the hourly alert-gate for 10+ days while the app served expired "JUNE 2026 SUMMER SALE" deals as current. Root-caused in `investigations/retro-parallel-outages-investigation.md` (retro action item #1). ADR-051's own comment had flagged the ambiguity ("a real no-specials store … indistinguishable").
**Decision**: Make "confirmed empty" a first-class outcome through the whole chain. (1) `postScrapeDetailed` in `scraperClient.ts` exposes the service success signal (`{ ok, intercepted }`); `postScrape` delegates, products path untouched. (2) The deals-scraper registry contract becomes `() => Promise<DealScrapeOutcome>` (`{ deals, confirmedEmpty }`, still never throws); `scrapeDutchieSpecials` asserts `confirmedEmpty: true` only when EVERY retry attempt had service success + a captured `GetSpecialMenuCards` op + a real `menuCards: []` (`pickMenuCards` distinguishes captured-empty from not-captured; cards present but all transform-skipped = shape drift = unconfirmed). `remedy-tulalip` (no GraphQL evidence) always reports false. (3) `ingestRun` forwards the flag (only when the normalized set is also empty) into the POST body and the ADR-047 seed artifact; new `'empty'` result is acceptable for the exit code. (4) `/api/ingest` validates the optional flag as strictly boolean (400 otherwise); `applyIngest` honors only `=== true`: clears `deals`, advances `lastFetchedAt`, `stale=false`, result `'empty'`, counts toward `accepted`/`lastScraperRun`. Unconfirmed empties keep the ADR-034 stale semantics byte-identically. Legacy `runScrapers` consumes `.deals` and deliberately ignores the flag.
**Rationale**: Honest Math (ADR-007/009): the fix demands positive evidence, not absence of deals — the ADR-051 3-attempt retry doubles as the race guard (a store must prove emptiness 3× in one run), so no extra requests. Clearing on evidence is more honest than both alternatives: serving expired deals forever (the bug) or tuning alert thresholds/mutes (masks the alarm without fixing the representation).
**Consequences**: A confirmed-empty store drops out of the feed (correct — no card beats a dead June promo), reads `ok` on `/api/data`, and stops redding the alert-gate. The registry contract change touches every deals scraper (one-line ripples). Non-Dutchie sources can't confirm emptiness and keep today's behavior — an honestly-dealless Cheerio store would still alert (acceptable: only Dutchie sources have shown this pattern). Commit-back seed clears too (same `applyIngest`).
**Testing**: 631 server tests green (+22: confirmed-empty detection incl. shape-drift/no-capture/failed-attempt/throw-blocks-confirmation guards, `postScrapeDetailed` matrix, ingestRun flag forwarding + contradictory-scraper defense, applyIngest clear/advance + truthy-junk rejection + deals-win-over-flag, ingestRoute strict-boolean 400, commit-back artifact clear, mt-vernon integration scenario). Full `npm run build` clean.

---

### ADR-084: Price vs own rolling median — the honest per-SKU discount (derivation-2.1 / D6 / FR13, fix6 keystone)
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-11
**Context**: fix6 established that a product's specialPrice-vs-basePrice discount % is a flat store/brand promo rate with no per-item signal (`investigations/fix6-basePrice-verdict.md`), so the deals feed's banner % is not an honest magnitude. The only honest discount is a SKU's current effective price vs its OWN rolling history. Epic-2 opens on this fact; Epic-3's in-app value card is gated on it. The (product_key, observedAt) indices designed into the ADR-077 substrate had no real consumer yet.
**Decision**: New pure fact `server/utils/priceVsOwnMedian.ts` (`buildPriceVsOwnMedianReport`) computes, per (product, option-label-verbatim) series, `pctVsMedian = (currentPrice − medianPrice) / medianPrice` where the median is over all observed days in a rolling window INCLUDING today. Constants ratified by Erik at dev-start: `ROLLING_WINDOW_DAYS=30`, `MIN_OBSERVED_DAYS=7` (distinct calendar days, not product-days), emit MOVERS ONLY (at-median series counted in `excluded[]` as `atOwnMedian`, not emitted). Reads history via a new BOUNDED SQLite time-range query `readWindowedObservations` (`WHERE observedAt >= ? ORDER BY product_key, observedAt` — the first real consumer of the 1.0 indices; sort guarantee lives in the SQL, not caller convention). Gap-tolerant via the 1.2 `walkPresenceAwareSeries` helper. Series key is the option label verbatim, NOT canonical weight — this fact makes no $/gram or cross-product claim, so the weight parser (and its "100mg"→0.1g mg-bogosity) is deliberately not imported. Effective price = `specialPrice ?? basePrice`, single-reduced at the runner boundary (decision F: the pure fn's narrowed input type has no base/special pair, no discount %, no potency — the breach does not compile). Runner writes `server/data/derived/price-vs-own-median.json` LAST (1.2.5 write-ordering) wrapped in the standard envelope.
**Rationale**: An always-on-special SKU's median already reflects the special, so its deal honestly reads ≈0% vs its own median — that is fix6's entire point, not an error. Movers-only keeps the artifact bounded (~230 rows vs ~2,060) while `excluded[]` still restates every suppressed series. Additive-only (NFR5): no route, no `data.json`, no client, no change to the other 8 facts' outputs.
**Consequences**: Ships suppression-heavy by design on ~18 days of history (~57% of series below a 7-day floor today) — correct, not a bug; coverage grows with accrual. The 2-day Dutchie outage shows as per-series gaps, never fabricated values. Redundant with the in-memory whole-file read the other facts share (accepted/temporary — establishes the bounded-read pattern Phase-2 extends). No alerting work — the envelope's `generatedAt` puts it under the existing 1.8 freshness check automatically.
**Testing**: 16 pure-fn tests (below/above/at-median, even-count median, floor + today-presence suppression, gap exclusion, window boundary, shuffled-input determinism, multi-option independence, unusable-price accounting, and `@ts-expect-error` compile gates that the base/special pair, `discountPct`, and `thc` are not assignable) + 4 windowed-reader tests (boundary inclusivity, sorted-on-read against out-of-order inserts, multi-product grouping, empty window) + 1 runner integration test (real below-median mover through the bounded read + sold-out-today exclusion). Cross-ref ADR-077, fix6.

---

### ADR-085: Cheapest-delivered INPUT fact — per-cell store offers + committed geo, no baked-in origin (derivation-2.2 / D7 / FR14)
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-12
**Context**: FR14 wants a "cheapest-delivered" value fact, but delivered cost = shelf price + round-trip gas, and ADR-057 made distance user-relative (measured from the visitor's GPS/ZIP, no fixed origin). So the derivation layer CANNOT honestly pre-pick a delivered winner — a farther store with a lower shelf price may win or lose once gas is added, and only the client knows where the shopper is. The layer must emit the INPUTS and let the client compose. Epic-3's delivered-value card is the consumer; the existing client `roundTripGasCost` (`client/src/utils/gasCost.ts`) is the verified-reusable composition surface.
**Decision**: New pure fact `server/utils/cheapestDelivered.ts` (`buildCheapestDeliveredReport`) is the structural twin of 1.4 (`disparityRollups.ts`): a pure function of `(Disparity[], StoreGeoLookup)` that reuses the already-computed `report.disparities` oracle and the already-built `geoLookup` straight from the runner — NO second DB read, no product re-grouping, no weight re-parse, no price re-reduction. Per disparity it emits ONE delivered cell `{ matchKey, displayName, category, weightGrams, storeOffers[] }` carrying EVERY qualifying store's offer `{ dispensaryId, price, pricePerGram, lat, lng }` — never just the cheapest, because the delivered winner is user-location-dependent (the `storeOffers` array is sorted cheapest-shelf-first for deterministic diffs ONLY, carrying no delivered-winner claim). `price` is `DisparityStore.price` verbatim (already `specialPrice ?? basePrice`, sold-out excluded upstream); `pricePerGram = r4(price / weightGrams)`; lat/lng from the geo lookup, `null` + counted in `missingGeoCount` when unresolved (1.4 discipline — never defaulted to 0,0, offer never dropped). NO origin/distance/gas baked in at derivation time (ADR-057). Runner writes `server/data/derived/cheapest-delivered.json` LAST (1.2.5 write-ordering), enveloped; appended to `$derivedFiles` in `derive-facts-local.ps1` (load-bearing — an unlisted artifact is written but never committed, then wiped by the next `git reset --hard`).
**Rationale**: All honesty gates are INHERITED, not re-implemented — a `Disparity` is by construction a same-product, same-canonical-weight, ≥2-distinct-store, sold-out-excluded cell (crossStoreValue.ts gates 1–5, `WEIGHT_BASED_CATEGORIES`, `EXCLUDED_FLAGS`, `canonicalWeightGrams` mg/count protection all ran once, upstream). Consuming that output means the gates hold by inheritance; this module imports no weight parser, so it cannot re-introduce the `"100mg"→0.1g` mg-bogosity (the inverse of 2.1, which avoided the parser because it made no weight claim — 2.2 relies on it but only through the gated `weightGrams` field). FR16 type-gate is structural: reusing the shared `Disparity`/`DisparityStore` type — which carries no base/special pair, no discount %, no potency — IS the decision-F posture (a compile-level `@ts-expect-error` test enforces it).
**Consequences**: Additive-only (NFR5): new module + test + runner block + ps1 line + this ADR; no route, no client, no `data.json`, no change to the other 9 facts' outputs (byte-identical given the same DB). No alerting work — the envelope's `generatedAt` puts it under the existing 1.8 freshness check automatically. Live grounding (committed `disparities.json`, 2026-07-12): ~305 cells / ~684 store-offers across 22 stores, all geo-resolved (`missingGeoCount ≈ 0`), zero Edible cells (Gate 5 screens the mg hazard before 2.2 ever sees the data). `pricePerGram` emitted as a convenience for Epic-3's "$/g delivered" card (reversible additive field).
**Testing**: 8 pure-fn tests (geo attach, null-geo counting for both absent + null-mapped stores, all-stores-lack-geo cell still emitted, all-offers-emitted sorted by price then dispensaryId, `pricePerGram` at 4dp with `price` passed through unrounded, matchKey/weightGrams cell sort, empty input, and `@ts-expect-error` compile gates that `thc`/`discountPct`/base+special pair are not assignable to `DisparityStore` or the emitted offer) + 2 runner integration assertions (real 2-store delivered cell through the reused oracle+geoLookup; mg-parse exclusion proof — an Edible/mg fixture never becomes a Disparity so it never reaches a cheapest-delivered cell, mirroring 2.1's sold-out seam proof). Cross-ref ADR-057, ADR-084, ADR-077.

---

### ADR-086: Regional price floor + availability gap — geo-clustered floors & category presence, no cross-product claim (derivation-2.3 / D8 / FR15)
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-12
**Context**: FR15, the final Epic-2 accrual fact, wants "regional value" — cheapest-near-me and where coverage holes are — without a dishonest cross-product or whole-category price claim. It needs geographic grouping of stores, then a like-for-like price floor within each region, plus a category-level presence/absence signal. The store coordinates and the disparity oracle already exist in the runner from 1.4/2.2; extraction-health already exists from 1.2.5/1.7.
**Decision**: New pure fact `server/utils/regionalPriceFloor.ts` (`buildRegionalPriceFloorReport`), the third structural sibling of 1.4/2.2: a pure function of `(Disparity[], StoreCategoryPresence[], StoreGeoLookup, Map<string,StoreHealthStatus>)` reusing already-in-scope runner inputs — NO DB read, no weight parse, no price reduction, no match-key derivation. **Clustering**: single-linkage agglomeration via union-find over pairwise haversine distance, threshold `<= CLUSTER_RADIUS_MILES` inclusive; connected components read off `find()` roots so the result is input-order insensitive; `clusterId` = lexicographically-first member, `memberDispensaryIds` sorted, centroid = 4dp member mean (convenience, no claim). `CLUSTER_RADIUS_MILES = 10` ratified by Erik at dev-start (over the 22 live geo-resolved stores → 4 honest regions: Bellingham 5 / Everett-corridor 11 / Skagit 5 / Oak Harbor 1 — a real single-store cluster; 20 mi chains everything into one mega-cluster, avoided). Haversine is LOCAL (R = 3958.8, numerically identical to `client/src/utils/distance.ts`) — the server cannot runtime-import client code (every server→client import in the tree is `import type` only; a value import is a new build-boundary crossing), and the client's ×1.3 road factor is deliberately NOT copied because clustering is a geometric grouping claim, not a delivered-cost claim (ADR-057 keeps the road factor client-side). **Floors**: per (cluster × Disparity), filter `storesCarrying` to cluster members; if ≥1 offer, emit `{ matchKey, displayName, category, weightGrams, floorPrice, floorDispensaryIds, storeCountInCluster }` where `floorPrice` = min offer price passed through verbatim (reconciles byte-for-byte with `disparities.json`) and `floorDispensaryIds` = ALL stores tied at the min, sorted (1.4 tie lesson). A single-store-in-cluster floor IS emitted with `storeCountInCluster: 1` explicit and counted in `singleStoreFloorCount` — the claim is "lowest observed in this cluster for this exact product+weight," true at n=1 and exactly what "cheapest X near me" needs. **Availability gaps**: `categoryUniverse` = data-derived sorted distinct categories over ALL presence records (never hardcoded); per cluster `categoriesPresent` = categories over member stores, `availabilityGaps` = universe − present. A gap is an ABSENCE assertion, dishonest if a member store's extraction silently broke — so a cluster with a `suspected-extraction-failure` member emits `availabilityGaps: []` + `gapsSuppressed: true` and is counted (Gate-4 spirit, 1.7 precedent); `insufficient-history` does NOT suppress (a young store's today-records are real). A store with null/absent geo is UNCLUSTERED + counted (`unclusteredStoreCount`), never defaulted to 0,0. Runner projects presence at the boundary `{ dispensaryId, category }` (decision F: the pure fn never sees a price/pair/potency — FR16 breach does not compile), reuses `report.disparities` + `geoLookup` + the 1.7 `storeStatus` map (no rebuild), and writes `server/data/derived/regional-price-floor.json` LAST (1.2.5 write-ordering), enveloped; appended to `$derivedFiles` in `derive-facts-local.ps1` (load-bearing).
**Rationale**: Gate 1 holds by INHERITANCE — a floor is a min WITHIN a same-product, same-canonical-weight `Disparity` cell, never across products or a category, and the report shape has NO field aggregating price across match-keys or a category, so the dishonest claim is inexpressible. Floors stay emitted even when gaps are suppressed because a floor is a POSITIVE claim (missing data can only make a min conservative, never a lie), whereas a gap is a negative/absence claim that a silent extraction hole would corrupt. Single-linkage with a conservative radius keeps regions honest without a routing API; the local-haversine/no-road-factor choice preserves the single user-facing distance formula (ADR-057) rather than seeding a second one server-side.
**Consequences**: Additive-only (NFR5): new module + test + runner block + ps1 line + this ADR; no route, no client, no `data.json`, no change to the other 10 facts' outputs (byte-identical given the same DB). No alerting work — the envelope's `generatedAt` puts it under the existing 1.8 freshness check automatically. Home `products.db` was empty at dev-time (pre-existing, 2.2 precedent) so the presence half could not be exercised over live data; verification bound to the committed-artifact clustering proof (radius sweep over the real 22 coordinates). Test fixture correction: the pre-written centroid test placed two stores at a diagonal `≈10.10 mi` (just OUTSIDE the ratified 10-mi radius) while asserting they merge — corrected the fixture's lng offset (+0.1→+0.05, `≈9.27 mi`) rather than bend the client-identical haversine or the ratified radius.
**Testing**: 14 pure-fn tests (boundary `<=` inclusive inside/outside, transitive chaining A–B–C, input-order insensitivity via whole-report `toEqual`, clusterId/centroid determinism, null/absent-geo store unclustered+counted contributing no floor/presence, floor min + full tie list, single-store-cluster floor + counter, Disparity-with-no-cluster-offers contributes no row, floor sort + verbatim price, availability gap for an absent category, suspected-member gap suppression with floors still emitted, insufficient-history non-suppression, empty inputs, and `@ts-expect-error` FR16 gates on `DisparityStore` + `StoreCategoryPresence`) + 2 runner integration assertions (envelope-shaped artifact with geoless fixtures → unclustered/zero-floor; Edible seam proof — 'Edible' reaches the category universe via presence yet yields zero floors because it never becomes a Disparity). Cross-ref ADR-057, ADR-084, ADR-085, ADR-077.

---

### ADR-087: In-app "Real price drops" surface — first client consumer of a derived value fact (derivation-3.2 / D6 / FR13)
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-13
**Context**: Derivation Epic 3 ("feed the surfaces") slice 2: put the flagship honest discount — a SKU priced below its OWN rolling median (`price-vs-own-median`, the fix6 keystone, the only discount magnitude that carries per-item signal) — in front of shoppers *in the app*. Two structural facts made this non-trivial: (1) the in-app feed is **store-keyed** (`DealFeed` renders one `DealCard` per store from `Dispensary.deals[]`) while this fact is **product-keyed** `(dispensaryId, productId, option)`, so it cannot honestly hang off an existing `DealCard` (a banner deal doesn't reference the product whose median we know; `deal-scope.json` deliberately carries no savings number); and (2) the runner produced `price-vs-own-median.json` but **no HTTP route served it** — Render served only `disparities`/`deal-scope`/`disparity-rollups`. Erik ratified (dev-start, Task 0) the flagship-only MVP as a standalone section (Option B), then requested a UX pass — Sally's `real-price-drops-surface.md` (final, ratified) fixed the placement + anatomy.
**Decision**: A served route + a standalone product-keyed section, both additive and fail-soft. **Route**: `GET /api/value/price-vs-own-median` (`priceVsOwnMedianRoute` + `EMPTY_PRICE_VS_OWN_MEDIAN_ENVELOPE` in `valueRoute.ts`), a line-for-line mirror of `disparitiesRoute` — reads the committed derived file through the shared `readDerived`, degrades a missing/malformed/wrong-shape file to a referentially-stable empty envelope (epoch `generatedAt`, never 500, never a raw-dataset read — the ADR-077 load-bearing rule). Registered before the SPA fallback; no Vite proxy change needed (the existing `'/api'` catch-all already covers it). **Client**: `useValueDrops` hook (sibling of `useDeals`, does NOT route through it) fetches on mount, fail-softs every path (non-ok / malformed / empty / abort) to `drops: []`; its exported pure `selectDrops` validates the envelope shape AND filters to `pctVsMedian < 0` so an above-median premium (the fact is movers-only, so `rows` carries both) can never surface as a "drop" (AC4). `ValueDrops.tsx` renders the ratified surface — `Card as="section"` + `<h2>` "Real price drops" + count + a **local** `<ul>` of ledger rows — joining each row's `dispensaryId` to `Dispensary.name` from the already-loaded `/api/data` list (one data load; drops are NOT distance-filtered so the FULL unfiltered list is the join source). Placed between the distance slider and the store feed via a one-line additive edit in `DealFeed.tsx`; `DealCard.tsx` untouched and the feed's one-listitem-per-store a11y count is preserved (the section is a sibling, not nested in the feed `<ul>`).
**Rationale**: Gate 2 is the whole point — the flat banner `discountPct` carries no per-item signal and is structurally absent from this fact/route/surface (a test asserts no `discountPct` on any served row and no verdict word reaches the DOM). ADR-009 no-verdicts: copy states the fact ("19% below its usual"), never "great deal"/"best"/"steal"/"you save"/"% off". Honest Math: the store-join **drops** a row whose store is absent or `failed`/`stale` rather than render it against a fabricated/unreliable store name; whole-number percent + 2dp dollars in mono/tabular figures; magnitude shown by weight-step within the single amber `--discount` hue (ADR-041), never a second color. Additive/fail-soft everywhere means the value surface can never break, block, or shift the store feed. **One deliberate refinement of the UX spec**: the "N today" count reflects the number of rows *actually rendered* (post store-join) rather than the report's `belowMedianCount` — a count that can never exceed the visible list is strictly more honest and never invites "why does it say 4 when I see 3?"; flagged for Erik, trivially reversible.
**Consequences**: Additive-only (NFR5) — new route + hook + component + CSS + client type `PriceDropRow`; `DealCard`/`useDeals`/`data.json`/`/api/data` untouched. `price-vs-own-median.json` is not yet committed (home `products.db` was empty 2026-07-12 — memory); the empty-envelope fallback serves the gap and the file republishes from the next home derive (already in `$derivedFiles`). Both live paths smoke-tested on the built server: empty file → HTTP 200 epoch envelope (no 500); a populated file in the dist derived dir → both rows served with real `generatedAt`, client would filter to the below-median drop. Two pre-existing `DealFeed` tests that asserted a global no-fetch were updated to mock `useValueDrops` (their intent — useDeals used the snapshot — is preserved). Client suite 581 green, server suite 682 green, real `tsc -b && vite build` + server `tsc` clean. Cross-ref ADR-077 (fail-soft/derived-serving), ADR-009 (no verdicts), ADR-041 (single discount hue), ADR-066 (legal), ADR-079 (SSR value-fact precedent), and `real-price-drops-surface.md` (binding UX). **Superseded in placement by ADR-088** (the standalone top-of-feed section is retired; drops now render inside each store's card).

---

### ADR-088: "Real price drops" relocated INTO each store's DealCard — co-located value strip, drops follow their store, union/no-drop-lost (derivation-3.3)
**Status**: Accepted
**Date**: 2026-07-13
**Context**: Erik's direct feedback on the ADR-087 surface: the standalone "Real price drops" block at the top of the feed reads as **unrelated** to the store cards that carry the link, address, distance, and deals — "the deal for the store should be with the cards so people see all information together." A drop showing "Blue Dream, 19% below usual — Green Fields" forced the shopper to hunt down the page for the Green Fields card to act on it. The fact is product-keyed `(dispensaryId, productId, option)` and the feed is store-keyed, but both share `dispensaryId`, so a store-join into the card is well-defined. Sally produced + Erik ratified the companion UX spec `real-price-drops-in-card.md` (supersedes the *placement* of `real-price-drops-surface.md`; keeps every honesty gate, copy, and number format). Two decisions were ratified at design time: (1) drops **follow their store** on the distance filter; (2) **union / no-drop-lost** — a fresh in-range store with a real drop but no banner deal still gets a card.
**Decision**: Retire the standalone `<ValueDrops>` section; render each store's drops as a labeled **"Real price drops" strip inside its `DealCard`**, under the banner deal grid. **New component** `ValueDropStrip.tsx` (+ exported pure `renderableDrops`) — a `<section aria-labelledby>` with an `<h3>` (a child of the card's `<h2>` store name) + a local `<ul>` of ledger rows; the store name leaves the row meta (only the option label remains) and the per-row accessible name (the card's `<h2>` already announces the store). **`DealCard`** gains a `drops` prop and renders the strip when present, with a hairline divider above it only when a banner deal grid precedes it (omitted on a drop-only/expired card). **`DealFeed`** consumes `useValueDrops`, groups drops by `dispensaryId`, gates out `failed`/`stale`-status stores, suppresses sub-1%-display/above-median rows (`renderableDrops`), and extends the carded-store set to the **union** of {live, recently-expired, drop-only} — a drop-only store (fresh, in-range, ≥1 renderable drop, not already carded, category-gated like expired cards) surfaces a "No current deals" card carrying just the strip. The served route + `useValueDrops` hook + `PriceDropRow` type are unchanged (still the data source).
**Rationale**: Co-location answers the actual user need (all of one store's info in one place) at zero honesty cost — every ADR-087 gate is inherited verbatim, and Gate 2 gets *more* load-bearing because banner deals and drops now share a card: they stay visually distinct (banner keeps `gma-deal-block` "N% off"; the drop keeps the `below its usual` ledger treatment in the single amber `--discount` hue). Distance-follows-store means the slider means one consistent thing everywhere (a drop past the slider is hidden with its store). Union/no-drop-lost was chosen over freezing the feed's store set specifically so no genuine drop is silently discarded — the trade-off (a few new store cards may appear) was ratified. The strip's rows stay a **nested labeled `<ul>`** (not bare divs): the mono "%"/"vs" mis-voices, so each row needs a real listitem to expose its spelled-out `aria-label` (inherited from the surface spec's ledger rows) — this is a nested list inside the card, never new items on the feed's top-level `<ul>`, so the one-`<li>`-per-store count is preserved.
**Consequences**: Client-only, additive, fail-soft (a broken drops fetch → `[]` → no strips, no drop-only cards, feed intact). Files: NEW `ValueDropStrip.tsx` (+test); modified `DealCard.tsx`/`DealFeed.tsx` (+tests) and `components.css` (re-scoped `gma-value-drops__*` to nest in a card + `.gma-value-drops--divided`); **deleted** `ValueDrops.tsx`/`ValueDrops.test.tsx`. Server untouched (682 green). Client suite **594** green, real `tsc -b && vite build` + server `tsc` clean. Since `price-vs-own-median.json` is still not committed (home DB empty 2026-07-12), the strip renders nothing live until the next home derive — the intended additive/fail-soft behavior. Cross-ref ADR-087 (the surface this relocates), ADR-009/041/066, and `real-price-drops-in-card.md` (binding UX).
